0x1b3a08 822 821 Specifies shadows for boxes, or inset shadows/glows.  The shadow shape will match the border box for the panel,so use border-radius to affect rounding. 0x1b8be8 703 702 Controls truncation of text that doesn't fit in a panel.  "clip" means to simply truncate (on char boundaries), "ellipsis" means to end with '...', and "shrink" means lower the font size to fit.
